```diff
diff --git a/tools/build_indexes.py b/tools/build_indexes.py
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/build_indexes.py
@@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Deterministically derive data indexes from immutable results/v1 records."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import json
+import re
+from collections import defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+UUID7 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$")
+PATH = re.compile(
+    r"^results/v1/[a-z0-9._-]{1,128}/[a-z0-9._-]{1,128}/"
+    r"(0|[1-9][0-9]*)/\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/([0-9a-f-]{36})\.json$"
+)
+
+INDEX_HEADER = (
+    "competition", "puzzle_type", "puzzle_id", "solution_length",
+    "solved_depth", "submission_id", "idempotency_key", "submitted_at",
+    "author_name", "kaggle_username", "run_id", "final_orientation",
+    "model_class", "output_dim", "model_sha256", "solver_commit",
+    "record_path",
+)
+
+
+class IndexError(ValueError):
+    pass
+
+
+def canonical(value: Any) -> str:
+    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
+                      separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
+
+
+def tsv_cell(value: Any) -> str:
+    """One logical cell; blocks TSV row injection and spreadsheet formulas."""
+    text = "" if value is None else str(value)
+    text = text.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("\t", "\\t")
+    text = text.replace("\r", "\\r").replace("\n", "\\n")
+    return "'" + text if text[:1] in "=+-@" else text
+
+
+def write_tsv(path: Path, header: tuple[str, ...], rows: list[tuple[Any, ...]]) -> None:
+    payload = "\t".join(header) + "\n"
+    payload += "".join("\t".join(tsv_cell(v) for v in row) + "\n" for row in rows)
+    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
+
+
+def row_from_record(relative: str, record: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
+    if set(record) != {"submission_id", "envelope"}:
+        raise IndexError("WRAPPER")
+    sid, envelope = record["submission_id"], record["envelope"]
+    match = PATH.fullmatch(relative)
+    if not isinstance(sid, str) or not UUID7.fullmatch(sid) or not match or match.group(2) != sid:
+        raise IndexError("PATH")
+    if not isinstance(envelope, dict):
+        raise IndexError("WRAPPER")
+
+    try:
+        author = envelope["author"]
+        solution = envelope["solution"]
+        profile = envelope["profile"]
+        model = envelope["model"]
+        manifest = model["manifest"]
+        row = (
+            envelope["competition"], envelope["puzzle_type"], envelope["puzzle_id"],
+            solution["length"], solution["solved_depth"], sid,
+            envelope["idempotency_key"], envelope["submitted_at"], author["name"],
+            author.get("kaggle_username", ""), envelope["run_id"],
+            envelope["orientation"]["final_orientation"], profile["model_class"],
+            manifest["output_dim"], model["sha256"], envelope["solver_commit"], relative,
+        )
+    except (KeyError, TypeError):
+        raise IndexError("PROVENANCE") from None
+
+    if not isinstance(row[6], str) or not isinstance(row[10], str):
+        raise IndexError("PROVENANCE")
+    return row
+
+
+def load_rows(results: Path) -> list[tuple[Any, ...]]:
+    rows: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
+    seen_submission: set[str] = set()
+    seen_idempotency: set[str] = set()
+    for path in sorted(results.rglob("*.json"), key=lambda p: p.as_posix()):
+        relative = path.relative_to(results.parent).as_posix()
+        try:
+            record = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
+            raise IndexError("JSON") from None
+        row = row_from_record(relative, record)
+        if row[5] in seen_submission:
+            raise IndexError("DUPLICATE_SUBMISSION")
+        if row[6] in seen_idempotency:
+            raise IndexError("DUPLICATE_IDEMPOTENCY")
+        seen_submission.add(row[5])
+        seen_idempotency.add(row[6])
+        rows.append(row)
+    return sorted(rows, key=lambda r: (r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[5], r[16]))
+
+
+def build(results: Path, out: Path) -> None:
+    rows = load_rows(results)
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+    write_tsv(out / "index.tsv", INDEX_HEADER, rows)
+
+    author_rows = sorted(
+        ((r[8], r[9], r[10], *r) for r in rows),
+        key=lambda r: (r[0], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5], r[6], r[8], r[19]),
+    )
+    write_tsv(
+        out / "by_author.tsv",
+        ("author_name", "kaggle_username", "run_id", *INDEX_HEADER),
+        author_rows,
+    )
+
+    groups: dict[tuple[Any, Any, Any], list[tuple[Any, ...]]] = defaultdict(list)
+    for row in rows:
+        groups[(row[0], row[1], row[2])].append(row)
+    best: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
+    for key in sorted(groups):
+        candidates = groups[key]
+        winner = min(candidates, key=lambda r: (r[3], r[4], r[5], r[16]))
+        best.append((*winner, len(candidates)))
+    write_tsv(out / "best_solutions.tsv", (*INDEX_HEADER, "candidate_count"), best)
+
+    runs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
+    for row in rows:
+        run_id = row[10]
+        immutable = {
+            "author_name": row[8],
+            "kaggle_username": row[9],
+            "model_sha256": row[14],
+            "solver_commit": row[15],
+        }
+        current = runs.setdefault(run_id, {"provenance": immutable, "records": []})
+        if current["provenance"] != immutable:
+            raise IndexError("RUN_CONFLICT")
+        current["records"].append({
+            "competition": row[0], "puzzle_type": row[1], "puzzle_id": row[2],
+            "solution_length": row[3], "solved_depth": row[4],
+            "submission_id": row[5], "idempotency_key": row[6],
+            "record_path": row[16],
+        })
+    for run in runs.values():
+        run["records"].sort(
+            key=lambda r: (r["competition"], r["puzzle_type"], r["puzzle_id"],
+                           r["solution_length"], r["submission_id"], r["record_path"])
+        )
+    (out / "runs.json").write_text(
+        canonical({"schema_version": 1, "runs": runs}) + "\n",
+        encoding="utf-8",
+        newline="\n",
+    )
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument("--results", type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    build(args.results, args.out)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

29:     "puzzle_type",
30:     "puzzle_id",
31:     "solution_length",
32:     "solved_depth",
33:     "submission_id",
34:     "idempotency_key",
35:     "submitted_at",
36:     "author_name",
37:     "kaggle_username",
38:     "run_id",
39:     "final_orientation",
40:     "model_class",
41:     "output_dim",
42:     "model_sha256",
43:     "solver_commit",
44:     "record_path",
45: )
46: 
47: 
48: class IndexBuildError(ValueError):
49:     """Stable fail-closed index build error."""
50: 
51: 
52: def canonical(value: Any) -> str:
53:     return json.dumps(
54:         value,
55:         ensure_ascii=False,
56:         sort_keys=True,
57:         separators=(",", ":"),
58:         allow_nan=False,
59:     )
60: 
61: 
62: def safe_segment(value: str) -> str:
63:     output = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9._-]+", "-", value.lower()).strip("-")
64:     if not output or len(output) > 128:
65:         raise IndexBuildError("PATH_SEGMENT")
66:     return output
67: 
68: 
69: def _required_dict(value: Any, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
70:     if not isinstance(value, dict):
71:         raise IndexBuildError(code)
72:     return value
73: 
74: 
75: def _required_string(value: Any, code: str) -> str:
76:     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
77:         raise IndexBuildError(code)
78:     return value
79: 
80: 
81: def _required_integer(value: Any, code: str) -> int:
82:     if type(value) is not int:
83:         raise IndexBuildError(code)
84:     return value
85: 
86: 
87: def tsv_cell(value: Any) -> str:
88:     """Encode one TSV cell without row or spreadsheet-formula injection."""
89:     text = "" if value is None else str(value)
90:     text = (
91:         text.replace("\\", "\\\\")
92:         .replace("\t", "\\t")
93:         .replace("\r", "\\r")
94:         .replace("\n", "\\n")
95:     )
96:     return "'" + text if text[:1] in "=+-@" else text
97: 
98: 
99: def tsv_payload(header: tuple[str, ...], rows: Iterable[tuple[Any, ...]]) -> bytes:
100:     lines = ["\t".join(header)]
101:     lines.extend("\t".join(tsv_cell(value) for value in row) for row in rows)
102:     return ("\n".join(lines) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
103: 
104: 
105: @dataclass(frozen=True)
106: class ResultRow:
107:     relative: str
108:     submission_id: str
109:     envelope: dict[str, Any]
110: 
111:     @property
112:     def competition(self) -> str:
113:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("competition"), "PROVENANCE")
114: 
115:     @property
116:     def puzzle_type(self) -> str:
117:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("puzzle_type"), "PROVENANCE")
118: 
119:     @property
120:     def puzzle_id(self) -> int:
121:         return _required_integer(self.envelope.get("puzzle_id"), "PROVENANCE")
122: 
123:     @property
124:     def solution(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
125:         return _required_dict(self.envelope.get("solution"), "PROVENANCE")
126: 
127:     @property
128:     def solution_length(self) -> int:
129:         return _required_integer(self.solution.get("length"), "PROVENANCE")
130: 
131:     @property
132:     def solved_depth(self) -> int:
133:         return _required_integer(self.solution.get("solved_depth"), "PROVENANCE")
134: 
135:     @property
136:     def idempotency_key(self) -> str:
137:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("idempotency_key"), "PROVENANCE")
138: 
139:     @property
140:     def submitted_at(self) -> str:
141:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("submitted_at"), "PROVENANCE")
142: 
143:     @property
144:     def author(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
145:         return _required_dict(self.envelope.get("author"), "PROVENANCE")
146: 
147:     @property
148:     def author_name(self) -> str:
149:         return _required_string(self.author.get("name"), "PROVENANCE")
150: 
151:     @property
152:     def kaggle_username(self) -> str:
153:         value = self.author.get("kaggle_username", "")
154:         if not isinstance(value, str):
155:             raise IndexBuildError("PROVENANCE")
156:         return value
157: 
158:     @property
159:     def run_id(self) -> str:
160:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("run_id"), "PROVENANCE")
161: 
162:     @property
163:     def orientation(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
164:         return _required_dict(self.envelope.get("orientation"), "PROVENANCE")
165: 
166:     @property
167:     def final_orientation(self) -> str:
168:         return _required_string(
169:             self.orientation.get("final_orientation"), "PROVENANCE"
170:         )
171: 
172:     @property
173:     def profile(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
174:         return _required_dict(self.envelope.get("profile"), "PROVENANCE")
175: 
176:     @property
177:     def model_class(self) -> str:
178:         return _required_string(self.profile.get("model_class"), "PROVENANCE")
179: 
180:     @property
181:     def model(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
182:         return _required_dict(self.envelope.get("model"), "PROVENANCE")
183: 
184:     @property
185:     def output_dim(self) -> int:
186:         manifest = _required_dict(self.model.get("manifest"), "PROVENANCE")
187:         return _required_integer(manifest.get("output_dim"), "PROVENANCE")
188: 
189:     @property
190:     def model_sha256(self) -> str:
191:         return _required_string(self.model.get("sha256"), "PROVENANCE")
192: 
193:     @property
194:     def solver_commit(self) -> str:
195:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("solver_commit"), "PROVENANCE")
196: 
197:     def index_tuple(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
198:         return (
199:             self.competition,
200:             self.puzzle_type,
201:             self.puzzle_id,
202:             self.solution_length,
203:             self.solved_depth,
204:             self.submission_id,
205:             self.idempotency_key,
206:             self.submitted_at,
207:             self.author_name,
208:             self.kaggle_username,
209:             self.run_id,
210:             self.final_orientation,
211:             self.model_class,
212:             self.output_dim,
213:             self.model_sha256,
214:             self.solver_commit,
215:             self.relative,
216:         )
217: 
218:     def primary_key(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
219:         return (
220:             self.competition,
221:             self.puzzle_type,
222:             self.puzzle_id,
223:             self.solution_length,
224:             self.submission_id,
225:             self.relative,
226:         )
227: 
228: 
229: def _decode_json(path: Path) -> Any:
230:     try:
231:         return json.loads(
232:             path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
233:             parse_constant=lambda _value: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
234:                 ValueError("non-finite JSON number")
235:             ),
236:         )
237:     except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
238:         raise IndexBuildError("JSON") from None
239: 
240: 
241: def _validate_path(relative: str, submission_id: str, envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
242:     match = RESULT_PATH.fullmatch(relative)
243:     if not match or not UUID7.fullmatch(submission_id):
244:         raise IndexBuildError("PATH")
245:     competition, puzzle_type, puzzle_id, day, path_id = match.groups()
246:     if path_id != submission_id:
247:         raise IndexBuildError("PATH_UUID")
248:     try:
249:         datetime.strptime(day, "%Y-%m-%d")
250:         derived = (
251:             safe_segment(_required_string(envelope.get("competition"), "PROVENANCE")),
252:             safe_segment(_required_string(envelope.get("puzzle_type"), "PROVENANCE")),
253:             str(_required_integer(envelope.get("puzzle_id"), "PROVENANCE")),
254:             _required_string(envelope.get("submitted_at"), "PROVENANCE")[:10],
255:         )
256:     except ValueError:
257:         raise IndexBuildError("PATH_DAY") from None
258:     if derived != (competition, puzzle_type, puzzle_id, day):
259:         raise IndexBuildError("PATH_DERIVATION")
260: 
261: 
262: def _record_paths(results: Path) -> list[Path]:
263:     if not results.exists():
264:         return []
265:     if not results.is_dir() or results.is_symlink():
266:         raise IndexBuildError("RESULTS_ROOT")
267:     output: list[Path] = []
268:     for path in results.rglob("*"):
269:         if path.is_symlink():
270:             raise IndexBuildError("RESULT_FILE")
271:         if path.is_dir():
272:             continue
273:         if not path.is_file():
274:             raise IndexBuildError("RESULT_FILE")
275:         relative = path.relative_to(results.parent).as_posix()
276:         if not RESULT_PATH.fullmatch(relative):
277:             raise IndexBuildError("RESULT_PATH")
278:         output.append(path)
279:     return sorted(output, key=lambda path: path.relative_to(results.parent).as_posix())
280: 
281: 
282: def load_rows(results: Path) -> list[ResultRow]:
283:     rows: list[ResultRow] = []
284:     seen_submission: set[str] = set()
285:     seen_idempotency: set[str] = set()
286:     for path in _record_paths(results):
287:         relative = path.relative_to(results.parent).as_posix()
288:         record = _decode_json(path)
289:         if (
290:             not isinstance(record, dict)
291:             or set(record) != {"submission_id", "envelope"}
292:             or not isinstance(record.get("submission_id"), str)
293:             or not isinstance(record.get("envelope"), dict)
294:         ):
295:             raise IndexBuildError("WRAPPER")
296:         submission_id = record["submission_id"]
297:         envelope = record["envelope"]
298:         _validate_path(relative, submission_id, envelope)
299:         row = ResultRow(relative, submission_id, envelope)
300:         # Force all indexed fields through their fail-closed type checks.
301:         row.index_tuple()
302:         if submission_id in seen_submission:
303:             raise IndexBuildError("DUPLICATE_SUBMISSION")
304:         if row.idempotency_key in seen_idempotency:
305:             raise IndexBuildError("DUPLICATE_IDEMPOTENCY")
306:         seen_submission.add(submission_id)
307:         seen_idempotency.add(row.idempotency_key)
308:         rows.append(row)
309:     return sorted(rows, key=ResultRow.primary_key)
310: 
311: 
312: def _run_provenance(row: ResultRow) -> dict[str, Any]:
313:     return {
314:         "author": row.author,
315:         "kaggle": _required_dict(row.envelope.get("kaggle"), "PROVENANCE"),
316:         "model": row.model,
317:         "hardware": _required_dict(row.envelope.get("hardware"), "PROVENANCE"),
318:         "solver_commit": row.solver_commit,
319:     }
320: 
321: 
322: def _run_record(row: ResultRow) -> dict[str, Any]:
323:     proof = _required_dict(row.envelope.get("proof"), "PROVENANCE")
324:     proof_hashes = {}
325:     for key in (
326:         "initial_state_sha256",
327:         "central_state_sha256",
328:         "generators_sha256",
329:         "reached_state_sha256",
330:     ):
331:         proof_hashes[key] = _required_string(proof.get(key), "PROVENANCE")
332:     return {
333:         "client_submission_id": _required_string(
334:             row.envelope.get("client_submission_id"), "PROVENANCE"
335:         ),
336:         "competition": row.competition,
337:         "idempotency_key": row.idempotency_key,
338:         "orientation": row.orientation,
339:         "profile": row.profile,
340:         "proof_hashes": proof_hashes,
341:         "puzzle_id": row.puzzle_id,
342:         "puzzle_type": row.puzzle_type,
343:         "record_path": row.relative,
344:         "runtime": _required_dict(row.envelope.get("runtime"), "PROVENANCE"),
345:         "solution": row.solution,
346:         "submission_id": row.submission_id,
347:         "submitted_at": row.submitted_at,
348:         "timings": _required_dict(row.envelope.get("timings"), "PROVENANCE"),
349:     }
350: 
351: 
352: def build_payloads(rows: list[ResultRow]) -> dict[str, bytes]:
353:     index_rows = [row.index_tuple() for row in rows]
354:     by_author_rows = [
355:         (
356:             row.author_name,
357:             row.kaggle_username,
358:             row.run_id,
359:             row.competition,
360:             row.puzzle_type,
361:             row.puzzle_id,
362:             row.solution_length,
363:             row.submission_id,
364:             row.idempotency_key,
365:             row.submitted_at,
366:             row.relative,
367:         )
368:         for row in sorted(
369:             rows,
370:             key=lambda row: (
371:                 row.author_name,
372:                 row.run_id,
373:                 row.competition,
374:                 row.puzzle_type,
375:                 row.puzzle_id,
376:                 row.solution_length,
377:                 row.submission_id,
378:                 row.relative,
379:             ),
380:         )
381:     ]
382: 
383:     groups: dict[tuple[str, str, int], list[ResultRow]] = {}
384:     for row in rows:
385:         groups.setdefault(
386:             (row.competition, row.puzzle_type, row.puzzle_id), []
387:         ).append(row)
388:     best_rows = []
389:     for group_key in sorted(groups):
390:         candidates = groups[group_key]
391:         winner = min(
392:             candidates,
393:             key=lambda row: (
394:                 row.solution_length,
395:                 row.solved_depth,
396:                 row.submission_id,
397:                 row.relative,
398:             ),
399:         )
400:         best_rows.append((*winner.index_tuple(), len(candidates)))
401: 
402:     runs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
403:     for row in rows:
404:         provenance = _run_provenance(row)
405:         current = runs.setdefault(
406:             row.run_id,
407:             {"provenance": provenance, "records": []},
408:         )
409:         if canonical(current["provenance"]) != canonical(provenance):
410:             raise IndexBuildError("RUN_CONFLICT")
411:         current["records"].append(_run_record(row))
412:     for run in runs.values():
413:         run["records"].sort(
414:             key=lambda record: (
415:                 record["competition"],
416:                 record["puzzle_type"],
417:                 record["puzzle_id"],
418:                 record["solution"]["length"],
419:                 record["submission_id"],
420:                 record["record_path"],
421:             )
422:         )
423: 
424:     return {
425:         "index.tsv": tsv_payload(INDEX_HEADER, index_rows),
426:         "by_author.tsv": tsv_payload(
427:             (
428:                 "author_name",
429:                 "kaggle_username",
430:                 "run_id",
431:                 "competition",
432:                 "puzzle_type",
433:                 "puzzle_id",
434:                 "solution_length",
435:                 "submission_id",
436:                 "idempotency_key",
437:                 "submitted_at",
438:                 "record_path",
439:             ),
440:             by_author_rows,
441:         ),
442:         "best_solutions.tsv": tsv_payload(
443:             (*INDEX_HEADER, "candidate_count"), best_rows
444:         ),
445:         "runs.json": (
446:             canonical({"schema_version": 1, "runs": runs}) + "\n"
447:         ).encode("utf-8"),
448:     }
449: 
450: 
451: def build(results: Path, out: Path) -> None:
452:     payloads = build_payloads(load_rows(results))
453:     out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
454:     temporary: list[tuple[Path, Path]] = []
455:     try:
456:         for name in OUTPUT_NAMES:
457:             destination = out / name
458:             temp = out / f".{name}.tmp"
459:             temp.write_bytes(payloads[name])
460:             temporary.append((temp, destination))
461:         for temp, destination in temporary:
462:             temp.replace(destination)
463:     finally:
464:         for temp, _destination in temporary:
465:             temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
466: 
467: 
468: def main() -> None:
469:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
470:     parser.add_argument("--results", type=Path, required=True)
471:     parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
472:     args = parser.parse_args()
473:     try:
474:         build(args.results.resolve(), args.out.resolve())
475:     except IndexBuildError as exc:
476:         raise SystemExit(f"INDEX_ERROR {exc}") from None
477: 
478: 
479: if __name__ == "__main__":
480:     main()
1: from __future__ import annotations
2: 
3: import copy
4: from hashlib import sha256
5: import importlib.util
6: import json
7: from pathlib import Path
8: import sys
9: 
10: import pytest
11: 
12: 
13: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
14: RESULTS_REPO = ROOT
15: SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
16:     "build_indexes", RESULTS_REPO / "tools" / "build_indexes.py"
17: )
18: assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
19: builder = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
20: sys.modules[SPEC.name] = builder
21: SPEC.loader.exec_module(builder)
22: GOLDENS = json.loads(
23:     (RESULTS_REPO / "tests" / "fixtures" / "golden.json").read_text(
24:         encoding="utf-8"
25:     )
26: )["cases"]
27: 
28: 
29: def envelope(name: str = "original_unicode_author") -> dict:
30:     return copy.deepcopy(
31:         next(case["envelope"] for case in GOLDENS if case["name"] == name)
32:     )
33: 
34: 
35: def record(name: str = "original_unicode_author") -> dict:
36:     item = envelope(name)
37:     return {"submission_id": item["client_submission_id"], "envelope": item}
38: 
39: 
40: def relative(item: dict) -> str:
41:     value = item["envelope"]
42:     return (
43:         f"results/v1/{builder.safe_segment(value['competition'])}/"
44:         f"{builder.safe_segment(value['puzzle_type'])}/{value['puzzle_id']}/"
45:         f"{value['submitted_at'][:10]}/{item['submission_id']}.json"
46:     )
47: 
48: 
49: def put(root: Path, item: dict) -> Path:
50:     path = root / relative(item)
51:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
52:     path.write_text(builder.canonical(item), encoding="utf-8")
53:     return path
54: 
55: 
56: def output_bytes(root: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]:
57:     return {name: (root / name).read_bytes() for name in builder.OUTPUT_NAMES}
58: 
59: 
60: def fingerprint(paths: list[Path]) -> list[str]:
61:     return [sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for path in paths]
62: 
63: 
64: def test_deterministic_rebuild_best_tie_and_raw_immutability(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
65:     first = record("original_unicode_author")
66:     second = record("empty_path_source")
67:     # Force a tie whose service id, not discovery order, chooses the winner.
68:     second["envelope"]["competition"] = first["envelope"]["competition"]
69:     second["envelope"]["puzzle_type"] = first["envelope"]["puzzle_type"]
70:     second["envelope"]["puzzle_id"] = first["envelope"]["puzzle_id"]
71:     second["envelope"]["submitted_at"] = first["envelope"]["submitted_at"]
72:     second["envelope"]["solution"]["length"] = first["envelope"]["solution"]["length"]
73:     second["envelope"]["solution"]["solved_depth"] = first["envelope"]["solution"]["solved_depth"]
74: 
75:     roots = [tmp_path / "forward", tmp_path / "reverse"]
76:     paths_forward = [put(roots[0], item) for item in (first, second)]
77:     paths_reverse = [put(roots[1], item) for item in (second, first)]
78:     before = fingerprint(paths_forward + paths_reverse)
79: 
80:     for root in roots:
81:         legacy = root / "data" / "legacy" / "keep.txt"
82:         legacy.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
83:         legacy.write_text("keep\n", encoding="utf-8")
84:         builder.build(root / "results", root / "data")
85:         initial = output_bytes(root / "data")
86:         builder.build(root / "results", root / "data")
87:         assert output_bytes(root / "data") == initial
88:         assert legacy.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "keep\n"
89: 
90:     assert output_bytes(roots[0] / "data") == output_bytes(roots[1] / "data")
91:     assert fingerprint(paths_forward + paths_reverse) == before
92:     winner = min(first["submission_id"], second["submission_id"])
93:     best = (roots[0] / "data" / "best_solutions.tsv").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
94:     assert winner in best
95:     assert best.endswith("\t2\n")
96:     for payload in output_bytes(roots[0] / "data").values():
97:         assert b"\r" not in payload
98: 
99: 
100: def test_duplicate_submission_and_idempotency_fail_closed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
101:     first = record()
102:     duplicate_id = copy.deepcopy(first)
103:     duplicate_id["envelope"]["competition"] = "other-competition"
104:     duplicate_id["envelope"]["submitted_at"] = "2026-07-30T00:00:00.000Z"
105:     put(tmp_path / "submission", first)
106:     put(tmp_path / "submission", duplicate_id)
107:     with pytest.raises(builder.IndexBuildError, match="DUPLICATE_SUBMISSION"):
108:         builder.build(
109:             tmp_path / "submission" / "results",
110:             tmp_path / "submission" / "data",
111:         )
112: 
113:     second = record("empty_path_source")
114:     second["envelope"]["idempotency_key"] = first["envelope"]["idempotency_key"]
115:     put(tmp_path / "idempotency", first)
116:     put(tmp_path / "idempotency", second)
117:     with pytest.raises(builder.IndexBuildError, match="DUPLICATE_IDEMPOTENCY"):
118:         builder.build(
119:             tmp_path / "idempotency" / "results",
120:             tmp_path / "idempotency" / "data",
121:         )
122: 
123: 
124: def test_run_conflict_and_maximal_run_provenance(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
125:     first = record()
126:     second = record("empty_path_source")
127:     second["envelope"]["run_id"] = first["envelope"]["run_id"]
128:     second["envelope"]["author"] = copy.deepcopy(first["envelope"]["author"])
129:     second["envelope"]["kaggle"] = copy.deepcopy(first["envelope"]["kaggle"])
130:     second["envelope"]["model"] = copy.deepcopy(first["envelope"]["model"])
131:     second["envelope"]["hardware"] = copy.deepcopy(first["envelope"]["hardware"])
132:     second["envelope"]["solver_commit"] = first["envelope"]["solver_commit"]
133:     put(tmp_path / "valid", first)
134:     put(tmp_path / "valid", second)
135:     builder.build(tmp_path / "valid" / "results", tmp_path / "valid" / "data")

Review complete: 6 focused tests pass. No malformed path bypass found; sorting, duplicate detection and best selection are deterministic.

Main findings:

- P1: `runs.json` does not treat `profile` as invariant run provenance.
- P1: indexer alone does not validate schema/proof/hash/replay/idempotency before deriving public indexes.
- P2: four output files are replaced sequentially, so a crash can leave mixed generations.
- P2: TSV sanitizer misses Unicode/control whitespace formula-prefix cases.

```

Review complete: 6 focused tests pass. No malformed path bypass found; sorting, duplicate detection and best selection are deterministic.
